# Document history

**Current API version:** 2014-10-31

The following table describes important changes in each release of the _Amazon RDS User Guide_ after May 2018. For notification about updates
to this documentation, you can subscribe to an RSS feed.

###### Note

You can filter new Amazon RDS features on the [What's New with Database?](https://aws.amazon.com/about-aws/whats-new/database/ "https://aws.amazon.com/about-aws/whats-new/database/") page. For
**Products**, choose **Amazon RDS**. Then search
using keywords such as `RDS Proxy` or `Oracle
 2023`.

| Change                                                                                                                                                             | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | Date               |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------ |
| Amazon RDS Extended Support version 5.7.44-RDS.20251212 for RDS for MySQL                                                                                          | The RDS Extended Support version 5.7.44-RDS.20251212 is now available for RDS for MySQL.<br>For more information, see [Amazon RDS Extended Support versions for RDS for MySQL](MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-extended-support-releases-version-5.7.44-20251212 "MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-extended-support-releases-version-5.7.44-20251212").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | December 12, 2025  |
| RDS for SQL Server Developer Edition.                                                                                                                              | RDS for SQL Server now supports SQL Server Developer Edition. For more<br>information, see [Working with SQL Server Developer Edition on RDS for SQL Server](sqlserver-dev-edition.md "sqlserver-dev-edition.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | December 2, 2025   |
| Amazon RDS supports MySQL 9.5 in the Database Preview environment                                                                                                  | MySQL 9.5 is now available in the Database Preview environment in the<br>US East (Ohio) AWS Region. For more information, see [MySQL version 9.5 in the Database Preview environment](MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-preview-environment-version-9-5 "MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-preview-environment-version-9-5").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | November 21, 2025  |
| Amazon RDS supports MySQL 8.4.7 and 8.0.44                                                                                                                         | You can now create Amazon RDS DB instances running MySQL 8.4.7 and 8.0.44. For more information, see [MySQL<br>on Amazon RDS versions](MySQL.Concepts.md "MySQL.Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | November 18, 2025  |
| Amazon RDS supports MariaDB versions 11.4.9, 10.11.15, and 10.6.24                                                                                                 | RDS for MariaDB now supports MariaDB versions 11.4.9, 10.11.15, and 10.6.24. For more information, see [MariaDB on Amazon RDS versions](MariaDB.Concepts.md "MariaDB.Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | November 18, 2025  |
| Amazon RDS supports additional storage volumes for Oracle and SQL Server                                                                                           | You can add up to three additional storage volumes to an RDS for Oracle or RDS for SQL Server<br>DB instance. Depending on your workload requirements, choose between gp3 or io2<br>storage for each volume. Scale your total storage up to 256 TiB per DB instance by<br>combining primary and additional storage volumes. For more information, see<br>[Additional storage volumes](CHAP_Storage.md#Welcome.AdditionalStorageVolumes "CHAP_Storage.md#Welcome.AdditionalStorageVolumes").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | November 14, 2025  |
| RDS for Oracle supports db.r7i preconfigured instance classes                                                                                                      | RDS for Oracle now supports db.r7i preconfigured instances for Enterprise Edition and Standard Edition 2 with BYOL.<br>These instance classes provide additional memory for Oracle database workloads. For example, db.r7i.xlarge.tpc2.mem2x<br>provides twice as much memory as a standard db.r7i.xlarge instance and enables multithreading.<br>For more information, see<br>[DB instance class support](Concepts.DBInstanceClass.md "Concepts.DBInstanceClass.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | October 2, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS for Db2 supports backing up a single Db2 database                                                                                                       | You can now use the `rdsadmin.backup_database` stored procedure to back up a<br>single Db2 database on an RDS for Db2 DB instance to an Amazon S3 bucket. For more<br>information, see [rdsadmin.backup_database](db2-sp-managing-databases.md#db2-sp-backup-database "db2-sp-managing-databases.md#db2-sp-backup-database").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | September 30, 2025 |
| Amazon RDS supports reserved DB instances for Amazon RDS for Db2                                                                                                   | You can now purchase reserved DB instances for RDS for Db2. For more information, see [Reserved DB instances for Amazon RDS](USER_WorkingWithReservedDBInstances.md "USER_WorkingWithReservedDBInstances.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | September 24, 2025 |
| Amazon RDS for Db2 supports Amazon S3 streaming to restore databases                                                                                               | RDS for Db2 now supports restoring a database directly from multiple database backup files in an Amazon S3 bucket. For more information, see [rdsadmin.restore_database](db2-sp-managing-databases.md#db2-sp-restore-database "db2-sp-managing-databases.md#db2-sp-restore-database").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | September 22, 2025 |
| Amazon RDS for Db2 supports Amazon S3 streaming to restore databases                                                                                               | RDS for Db2 now supports restoring a database directly from multiple database backup files in an Amazon S3 bucket. For more information, see [rdsadmin.restore_database](db2-sp-managing-databases.md#db2-sp-restore-database "db2-sp-managing-databases.md#db2-sp-restore-database").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | September 22, 2025 |
| Amazon RDS supports MySQL 9.4 in the Database Preview environment                                                                                                  | MySQL 9.4 is now available in the Database Preview environment in the<br>US East (Ohio) AWS Region. For more information, see [MySQL version 9.4 in the Database Preview environment](MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-preview-environment-version-9-4 "MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-preview-environment-version-9-4").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | September 19, 2025 |
| Amazon RDS Extended Support version 5.7.44-RDS.20250818 for RDS for MySQL                                                                                          | The RDS Extended Support version 5.7.44-RDS.20250818 is now available for RDS for MySQL.<br>For more information, see [Amazon RDS Extended Support versions for RDS for MySQL](MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-extended-support-releases-version-5.7.44-20250508 "MySQL.Concepts.md#mysql-extended-support-releases-version-5.7.44-20250508").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | September 15, 2025 |
| RDS for Oracle supports db.m7i and db.r7i for SE2 LI                                                                                                               | RDS for Oracle Standard Edition 2 (SE2) using the License Included (LI) model supports the db.m7i and db.r7i<br>instance classes in the Asia Pacific (Thailand) and Mexico (Central) Region. For more information, see<br>[Oracle DB instance classes](Oracle.Concepts.md "Oracle.Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | August 28, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS is available in the Asia Pacific (New Zealand) Region                                                                                                   | Amazon RDS is now available in the Asia Pacific (New Zealand) Region. For more information, see [Regions and Availability<br>Zones](Concepts.md "Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | August 28, 2025    |
| RDS for Oracle and RDS Custom for Oracle support bare metal instance classes                                                                                       | RDS for Oracle and RDS Custom for Oracle now support bare metal instance classes for Enterprise<br>Edition with BYOL. Bare metal instances give your applications direct access to<br>the processor and memory resources of the underlying server. For more<br>information, see [DB instance<br>classes](Concepts.md "Concepts.md") and [DB instance class support for RDS Custom for Oracle](custom-oracle-feature-support.md#custom-reqs-limits.instances "custom-oracle-feature-support.md#custom-reqs-limits.instances").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | August 27, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS supports MariaDB 11.8                                                                                                                                   | You can now create Amazon RDS DB instances running MariaDB version 11.8. For more<br>information, see [MariaDB on<br>Amazon RDS versions](MariaDB.Concepts.md "MariaDB.Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | August 25, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS for Oracle supports ECDSA cipher suites and ECC certificates                                                                                            | Amazon RDS for Oracle now supports Elliptic Curve Digital Signature Algorithm (ECDSA) cipher suites for the SSL and OEM Agent options.<br>These cipher suites are compatible with ECC certificates. For more information, see [Certificate compatibility with cipher suites](Appendix.Oracle.Options.md#Appendix.Oracle.Options.SSL.CertificateCompatibility "Appendix.Oracle.Options.md#Appendix.Oracle.Options.SSL.CertificateCompatibility") for the SSL option and [Certificate compatibility with cipher suites](Oracle.Options.md#Oracle.Options.OEMAgent.CertificateCompatibility "Oracle.Options.md#Oracle.Options.OEMAgent.CertificateCompatibility") for the OEM Agent option.                                                                                | August 25, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS for Db2 supports cross-Region read-only replicas                                                                                                        | You can now create RDS for Db2 cross-Region read-only replicas. For more information, see [Working with replicas for Amazon RDS for Db2](db2-replication.md "db2-replication.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | August 22, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS supports MariaDB 11.4.8, 10.11.14, and 10.6.23                                                                                                          | You can now create Amazon RDS DB instances running MariaDB version 11.4.8, 10.11.14, and 10.6.2.<br>For more information, see [MariaDB<br>on Amazon RDS versions](MariaDB.Concepts.md "MariaDB.Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | August 14, 2025    |
| Amazon RDS Custom service-linked role policy updated                                                                                                               | The AmazonRDSCustomServiceRolePolicy managed policy has been updated to comply with EBS CopySnapshot authorization behavior.<br>The update removes `ec2:CopySnapshot` from one statement and<br>adds two new statements for source and destination snapshot permissions.<br>For more information, see [Updates to AWS managed policies for Amazon RDS](rds-managed-policy-updates.md "rds-managed-policy-updates.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | August 7, 2025     |
| Amazon RDS supports MySQL 8.4.6 and 8.0.43                                                                                                                         | You can now create Amazon RDS DB instances running MySQL 8.4.6 and 8.0.43. MySQL 8.4.6 includes the addition of the rds_security_audit database plugin, an internal RDS MySQL plugin that collects security-related metrics. For more information, see [MySQL<br>on Amazon RDS versions](MySQL.Concepts.md "MySQL.Concepts.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | August 1, 2025     |
| RDS for Oracle supports the db.m6in and db.r6in DB instance classes                                                                                                | You can use the db.m6in and db.r6in DB instance classes with RDS for Oracle with the Bring Your Own License (BYOL) model.<br>For more information, see [RDS for Oracle DB instance classes](Concepts.DBInstanceClass.md "Concepts.DBInstanceClass.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | July 29, 2025      |
| Zero-ETL integrations from RDS for PostgreSQL and RDS for Oracle are now available                                                                                 | You can now create Zero-ETL integrations with RDS for PostgreSQL and RDS for Oracle as the<br>source. | Amazon RDS for Db2 supports group authorization using Kerberos                                                                                                     | You can now perform group authorization using Kerberos with your self-managed Active Directory. | Zero-ETL integrations with Amazon SageMaker AI is generally available                                                                                              | Zero-ETL integrations make transactional data<br>available in an Amazon SageMaker lakehouse within seconds of it being written to an RDS for MySQL DB instance.<br>For more information, see [Working with Amazon RDS<br>zero-ETL integrations](zero-etl.md "zero-etl.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | June 30, 2025      |

| End-of-life information for Performance Insights                                                                                                                   | AWS has announced the end-of-life date for Performance Insights: November 30, 2025. | Amazon RDS for SQL Server supports local instance storage for the tempdb<br>database                                                                               | You can now launch Amazon RDS for SQL Server on Amazon EC2 db.r5d and db.m5d instance<br>types with the tempdb database configured to use an instance store. For more<br>information on the pg_cron extension, see [Scheduling<br>maintenance with the PostgreSQL pg_cron extension](PostgreSQL_pg_cron.md "PostgreSQL_pg_cron.md").                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | January 12, 2021   |
| Amazon RDS supports publishing the Oracle Management Agent log to<br>Amazon CloudWatch Logs                                                                        | The Oracle Management Agent log consists of emctl.log, emdctlj.log,<br>gcagent.log, gcagent_errors.log, emagent.nohup, and secure.log.
